CHANGELOG entry (test-only — grouped under the T2 entry)\n\n#### TDD\n```\nRED:     public_entry_composes_provider_and_message_for_both_roles() — import from '../src/index.js'; render provider(custom user glyph)+2 messages; expect(frame).toContain(customGlyph); expect(frame).toContain(\"✦ \"); expect(frame).toContain both contents (RED via intentionally-wrong import first, then GREEN with real path)\nGREEN:   Fix import to ../src/index.js — passes with no production change\nREFACTOR: None expected\nVERIFY:  pnpm vitest run tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx\n```\n\n#### Concurrency tests\n(none — single-threaded)\n\n#### Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `pnpm vitest run tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx` exits 0 — imports resolve ONLY via `src/index.js`\n- [ ] Pass: quality — `pnpm lint` exits 0; `wc -l tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx` <= 500\n\n#### DoD (Definition of Done)\n- [ ] `pnpm gates` exits 0\n\n---\n\n## Phase 3: Benchmark + example (wiring triad closure)\n\n**Objective:** Render-benchmark harness with statistical protocol + committed baseline JSON +\nrunnable example — the milestone's runtime-observability pillar.\n\n### T3.1 — Benchmark harness + committed baseline\n\n#### Objective\n`benchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx` (workload) + `benchmarks/run.ts` (runner): renders a\n100-message thread + 300-token streaming append into the last message via ink-testing-library;\ncaptures frames + mean/peak/std-dev ms-per-frame over 1 warmup + ≥ 3 measured runs; writes\n`docs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json` and prints a console table.\n\n#### Why this step (action + reasoning)\n\n1. **What:** Port react-ink's harness pattern to the M0 scale, upgraded with the statistical\n   protocol (≥ 3 runs, mean ± std dev) and JSON persistence.\n2. **Why now:** The cycle owner requires benchmark DATA as acceptance evidence; ADR D6/D9 lock\n   the harness design + data-only posture. It lands after ChatMessage exists (it renders it) and\n   before CI (which smoke-runs it).\n\n#### Evidence\n- Harness prior art: `knowledge-base/references/assistant-ui/packages/react-ink/benchmarks/run.ts:19`\n  (tsx spawn), `benchmarks/long-thread.bench.tsx:205-230` (frame-time sampling via\n  `performance.now()` + `stdout.frames` deltas), `:272-341` (console reporting).\n- Statistical protocol: `rules/analysis-golden-rule.md § 3` (≥ 3 runs, mean ± std dev, units +\n  methodology + hardware).\n- Artifact requirement: `rules/public-copy.md § 4` (reproducible benchmark artifact under\n  `docs/benchmarks/`).\n\n#### Files to edit\n```\nbenchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx — (NEW) workload + sampling + stats\nbenchmarks/run.ts                 — (NEW) discovers *.bench.tsx, spawns via tsx, forwards exit code\ndocs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json — (NEW) committed baseline (generated, then committed)\npackage.json                      — bench + bench:smoke scripts (declared in T0.1, verified here)\n```\n\n#### Deep file dependency analysis\n- `benchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx`: imports `{ ChatMessage, TheoTUIProvider }` from\n  `../src/index.js` (production caller — wiring pillar a) + `ink-testing-library` render.\n- `benchmarks/run.ts`: fs/child_process only; forwards non-zero exit (fail-loud —\n  `rules/error-handling.md § 2`).\n- `docs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json`: consumed by future `/analysis` +\n  M1 regression comparisons; `.prettierignore`d (T0.2) so format never rewrites data.\n\n#### Deep Dives\n- Workload: build 100 `ChatMessage` elements (alternating roles) under one provider; then 300\n  sequential `rerender()` calls appending a token to the last message's text (streaming\n  simulation, no timers — synchronous re-render loop keeps the bench deterministic-ish and fast).\n- Metrics per run: `frames` (stdout.frames.length delta), `mean_ms_per_frame`,\n  `peak_ms_per_frame`; sampled via `performance.now()` around rerender batches — honest\n  heuristic, same as prior art, noted in JSON `methodology` field.\n- Protocol: 1 warmup (discarded) + N=5 measured runs (exceeds the ≥ 3 floor); aggregate\n  `mean ± std_dev` per metric.\n- JSON schema (exact fields): `{ benchmark, date, node_version, hardware: {cpu, cores},\n  workload: {messages, streamed_tokens}, protocol: {warmup_runs, measured_runs},\n  runs: [{frames, mean_ms_per_frame, peak_ms_per_frame}], aggregate: {frames_mean,\n  mean_ms_per_frame: {mean, std_dev}, peak_ms_per_frame: {mean, std_dev}}, methodology }`.\n- `--smoke` flag: 1 measured run, NO file write — CI proof-of-execution (ADR D9).\n- Edge case: if `stdout.frames` yields 0 new frames for a batch (Ink coalescing), the sampler\n  skips the empty delta (prior-art behavior, `long-thread.bench.tsx:210` `if (newFrames <= 0) return`).\n\n#### Pseudo-code / Signatures\n```pseudocode\nasync function runOnce(): RunMetrics\n  instance = render(<App messages=100 />)         -- inside ink-testing-library\n  t0 = performance.now(); framesSeen = instance.stdout.frames.length\n  for token in 1..300:\n    rerender(<App messages=100 streamedTokens=token />)\n    sample(instance, now())                        -- frame-delta sampling\n  return { frames, mean_ms_per_frame, peak_ms_per_frame }\n\nmain: warmup(); runs = repeat(5, runOnce); writeJSON(aggregate(runs)) unless --smoke\n\n# Example aggregate output (real numbers land at implement)\n{ \"mean_ms_per_frame\": { \"mean\": 3.1, \"std_dev\": 0.4 }, ... }\n```\n\n#### Tasks\n1. { readFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\n\ninterface BenchBaseline {\n  benchmark: string;\n  date: string;\n  node_version: string;\n  hardware: { cpu: string; cores: number };\n  workload: { messages: number; streamed_tokens: number };\n  protocol: { warmup_runs: number; measured_runs: number };\n  runs: {\n    frames: number;\n    mean_ms_per_frame: number;\n    peak_ms_per_frame: number;\n  }[];\n  aggregate: {\n    frames_mean: number;\n    mean_ms_per_frame: { mean: number; std_dev: number };\n    peak_ms_per_frame: { mean: number; std_dev: number };\n  };\n  methodology: string;\n}\n\n// T3.1 (plan ADR D6/D9): the committed baseline is the milestone's\n// runtime-evidence artifact. EC-2: NaN is typeof number — Number.isFinite is\n// the real oracle against a corrupt baseline poisoning future regressions.\ndescribe(\"benchmark baseline (T3.1)\", () => {\n  it(\"benchmark_baseline_json_exists_with_statistical_protocol\", () => {\n    const baseline = JSON.parse(\n      readFileSync(\n        new URL(\n          \"../docs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json\",\n          import.meta.url,\n        ),\n        \"utf8\",\n      ),\n    ) as BenchBaseline;\n\n    expect(baseline.protocol.measured_runs).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);\n    expect(baseline.runs.length).toBe(baseline.protocol.measured_runs);\n    expect(baseline.protocol.warmup_runs).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);\n\n    for (const run of baseline.runs) {\n      expect(run.frames).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n      expect(Number.isFinite(run.mean_ms_per_frame)).toBe(true);\n      expect(Number.isFinite(run.peak_ms_per_frame)).toBe(true);\n    }\n    expect(Number.isFinite(baseline.aggregate.frames_mean)).toBe(true);\n    expect(Number.isFinite(baseline.aggregate.mean_ms_per_frame.mean)).toBe(\n      true,\n    );\n    expect(Number.isFinite(baseline.aggregate.mean_ms_per_frame.std_dev)).toBe(\n      true,\n    );\n    expect(Number.isFinite(baseline.aggregate.peak_ms_per_frame.mean)).toBe(\n      true,\n    );\n    expect(Number.isFinite(baseline.aggregate.peak_ms_per_frame.std_dev)).toBe(\n      true,\n    );\n\n    expect(baseline.methodology.length > 0).toBe(true);\n    expect(baseline.node_version.length > 0).toBe(true);\n    expect(baseline.hardware.cores).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(baseline.workload.messages).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(baseline.workload.streamed_tokens).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n  });\n});\n","numLines":69,"startLine":1,"totalLines":69}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5c1478a7-0662-4695-ae24-82858d5ac8c9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/paulo/Projetos/usetheo/theokit-tools/theokit-tui","sessionId":"56c33c5e-7528-4d27-b50f-010a5e093ccb","version":"2.1.187","gitBranch":"develop"}

\"vitest\";\n\n// Public-entry surface contract (plan T0.2, grows with T1.1/T2.1).\n// src/index.ts is the composition root — the ONLY public surface of the package.\ndescribe(\"public entry surface (T0.2)\", () => {\n  it(\"public_entry_exposes_version_constant\", async () => {\n    const mod = await import(\"../src/index.js\");\n    expect(mod.VERSION).toBe(\"0.0.0\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"public_entry_exposes_theme_surface\", async () => {\n    const mod = await import(\"../src/index.js\");\n    expect(typeof mod.TheoTUIProvider).toBe(\"function\");\n    expect(typeof mod.useTheoTheme).toBe(\"function\");\n    expect(mod.defaultTheme.role.user.glyph).toBe(\"> \");\n    expect(mod.defaultTheme.role.assistant.glyph).toBe(\"✦ \");\n  });\n\n  it(\"public_entry_exposes_chat_message\", async () => {\n    const mod = await import(\"../src/index.js\");\n    expect(typeof mod.ChatMessage).toBe(\"function\");\n  });\n});\nimport { readFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\n\n// Packaging contract for @theokit/tui (plan T0.1, ADR D8).\n// The manifest IS the M0 deliverable: these invariants protect consumers\n// from silent packaging regressions (a stray CJS field, a widened peer set).\ninterface PackageManifest {\n  name: string;\n  type: string;\n  license: string;\n  sideEffects: boolean;\n  private?: unknown;\n  exports: Record<string, Record<string, string> | undefined>;\n  files: string[];\n  engines: Record<string, string>;\n  peerDependencies: Record<string, string>;\n  dependencies: Record<string, string>;\n}\n\nconst pkg = JSON.parse(\n  readFileSync(new URL(\"../package.json\", import.meta.url), \"utf8\"),\n) as PackageManifest;\n\ndescribe(\"package manifest contract (T0.1)\", () => {\n  it(\"package_manifest_declares_esm_only_types_first_exports\", () => {\n    expect(pkg.type).toBe(\"module\");\n    expect(Object.keys(pkg.exports)).toEqual([\".\"]);\n    const dot = pkg.exports[\".\"] ?? {};\n    // \"types\" MUST precede \"default\" — Node/TS resolve conditions in order.\n    expect(Object.keys(dot)).toEqual([\"types\", \"default\"]);\n    expect(dot[\"types\"]).toBe(\"./dist/index.d.ts\");\n    expect(dot[\"default\"]).toBe(\"./dist/index.js\");\n    expect(pkg.files).toEqual([\"dist\"]);\n  });\n\n  it(\"package_manifest_declares_react_as_only_peer_and_ink_as_dependency\", () => {\n    expect(Object.keys(pkg.peerDependencies)).toEqual([\"react\"]);\n    expect(pkg.peerDependencies.react).toBe(\"^18.0.0 || ^19.0.0\");\n    expect(pkg.dependencies.ink).toMatch(/^\\^5/);\n  });\n\n  it(\"package_manifest_declares_apache2_license_and_node20_floor\", () => {\n    expect(pkg.name).toBe(\"@theokit/tui\");\n    expect(pkg.license).toBe(\"Apache-2.0\");\n    expect(pkg.engines.node).toBe(\">=20\");\n    expect(pkg.sideEffects).toBe(false);\n    expect(pkg.private).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n});\n---BASELINE---\n{\n  \"benchmark\": \"chat-message-render\",\n  \"date\": \"2026-07-06T11:15:11.057Z\",\n  \"node_version\": \"v22.22.2\",\n {"parentUuid":"c6d958e1-7c0e-4e8f-a01d-a2ed665d653f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"04491864-2bd1-40d0-aa60-0400bf7cca3c","agentId":"a64aa7b00cab1e654","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DdgViteZGCVWj2mQCu95V2","type":"tool_result","content":"import { spawnSync } from \"node:child_process\";\nimport { readdirSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { dirname, join } from \"node:path\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\n\n// Bench runner (plan T3.1): discovers *.bench.tsx and spawns each via tsx,\n// forwarding CLI args (--smoke) and exit codes — fail loud, never swallow\n// (rules/error-handling.md § 2). Pattern borrowed from the react-ink analog.\nconst benchDir = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst files = readdirSync(benchDir).filter((f) => f.endsWith(\".bench.tsx\"));\n\nif (files.length === 0) {\n  console.error(\"benchmarks/run.ts: no *.bench.tsx files found — aborting\");\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\nconst args = process.argv.slice(2);\nfor (const file of files) {\n  const result = spawnSync(\n    \"pnpm\",\n    [\"exec\", \"tsx\", join(benchDir, file), ...args],\n    {\n      stdio: \"inherit\",\n    },\n  );\n  if (result.error) {\n    console.error(\n      `benchmarks/run.ts: failed to spawn ${file}: ${result.error.message}`,\n    );\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  if (result.status !== 0) {\n    process.exit(result.status ?? 1);\n  }\n}\nimport { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { cpus } from \"node:os\";\nimport { dirname, join } from \"node:path\";\nimport { performance } from \"node:perf_hooks\";\nimport { fileURLToPath } from \"node:url\";\n\nimport { Box } from \"ink\";\nimport { render } from \"ink-testing-library\";\n\nimport { ChatMessage, TheoTUIProvider } from \"../src/index.js\";\n\n// M0 render benchmark (plan T3.1, ADR D6/D9 — data-only, no threshold gate).\n// Workload: 100-message static thread + 300-token streaming append into the\n// last assistant message. Metrics: frame count + mean/peak ms-per-frame.\n// Protocol: 1 discarded warmup + 5 measured runs, mean ± std dev.\nconst N_MESSAGES = 100;\nconst N_TOKENS = 300;\nconst WARMUP_RUNS = 1;\nconst MEASURED_RUNS = 5;\n\nconst smoke = process.argv.includes(\"--smoke\");\n\nconst tick = async (): Promise<void> =>\n  new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 0));\n\nconst round = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000;\n\ninterface RunMetrics {\n  frames: number;\n  mean_ms_per_frame: number;\n  peak_ms_per_frame: number;\n}\n\nfunction App({ streamed }: { streamed: string }) {\n  const history = Array.from({ length: N_MESSAGES }, (_, i) => (\n    <ChatMessage key={i} role={i % 2 === 0 ? \"user\" : \"assistant\"}>\n      {`message ${i} — the quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog`}\n    </ChatMessage>\n  ));\n  return (\n    <TheoTUIProvider>\n      <Box flexDirection=\"column\">\n        {history}\n        <ChatMessage role=\"assistant\">{streamed}</ChatMessage>\n      </Box>\n    </TheoTUIProvider>\n  );\n}\n\nasync function runOnce(): Promise<RunMetrics> {\n  const frameTimes: number[] = [];\n  const instance = render(<App streamed=\"\" />);\n  await tick();\n  let lastSeen = instance.frames.length;\n  let lastTime = performance.now();\n  let text = \"\";\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < N_TOKENS; i++) {\n    text += \"tok \";\n    instance.rerender(<App streamed={text} />);\n    await tick();\n    const now = performance.now();\n    const newFrames = instance.frames.length - lastSeen;\n    if (newFrames > 0) {\n      // Wall time distributed evenly across the frames flushed since the last\n      // sample — same honest heuristic as the react-ink prior art.\n      const perFrame = (now - lastTime) / newFrames;\n      for (let k = 0; k < newFrames; k++) {\n        frameTimes.push(perFrame);\n      }\n      lastTime = now;\n      lastSeen = instance.frames.length;\n    }\n  }\n  instance.unmount();\n\n  const frames = frameTimes.length;\n  if (frames === 0) {\n    // EC-2: never serialize NaN/Infinity aggregates from an empty sample.\n    console.error(\"bench: 0 frames captured — aborting (EC-2 guard)\");\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  const mean = frameTimes.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / frames;\n  const peak = Math.max(...frameTimes);\n  return {\n    frames,\n    mean_ms_per_frame: round(mean),\n    peak_ms_per_frame: round(peak),\n  };\n}\n\nconst stats = (values: number[]): { mean: number; std_dev: number } => {\n  const mean = values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length;\n  const variance =\n    values.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) ** 2, 0) / values.length;\n  return { mean: round(mean), std_dev: round(Math.sqrt(variance)) };\n};\n\nconst fmt = (label: string, r: RunMetrics): string =>\n  `${label.padEnd(12)} frames=${String(r.frames).padStart(4)}  mean=${r.mean_ms_per_frame\n    .toFixed(3)\n    .padStart(8)}ms  peak=${r.peak_ms_per_frame.toFixed(3).padStart(8)}ms`;\n\nconsole.log(\n  `chat-message bench — ${N_MESSAGES} messages + ${N_TOKENS} streamed tokens` +\n    (smoke ? \" [SMOKE: 1 run, no file write]\" : \"\"),\n);\n\nfor (let i = 0; i < WARMUP_RUNS; i++) {\n  const w = await runOnce();\n  console.log(fmt(`warmup #${i + 1}`, w) + \"  (discarded)\");\n}\n\nconst measured = smoke ? 1 : MEASURED_RUNS;\nconst runs: RunMetrics[] = [];\nfor (let i = 0; i < measured; i++) {\n  const r = await runOnce();\n  runs.push(r);\n  console.log(fmt(`run #${i + 1}`, r));\n}\n\nconst aggregate = {\n  frames_mean: round(runs.reduce((a, r) => a + r.frames, 0) / runs.length),\n  mean_ms_per_frame: stats(runs.map((r) => r.mean_ms_per_frame)),\n  peak_ms_per_frame: stats(runs.map((r) => r.peak_ms_per_frame)),\n};\n\nconsole.log(\"=== aggregate ===\");\nconsole.log(\n  `frames_mean=${aggregate.frames_mean}  ` +\n    `mean=${aggregate.mean_ms_per_frame.mean}±${aggregate.mean_ms_per_frame.std_dev}ms  ` +\n    `peak=${aggregate.peak_ms_per_frame.mean}±${aggregate.peak_ms_per_frame.std_dev}ms`,\n);\n\nif (!smoke) {\n  const baseline = {\n    benchmark: \"chat-message-render\",\n    date: new Date().toISOString(),\n    node_version: process.version,\n    hardware: {\n      cpu: cpus()[0]?.model ?? \"unknown\",\n      cores: cpus().length,\n    },\n    workload: { messages: N_MESSAGES, streamed_tokens: N_TOKENS },\n    protocol: { warmup_runs: WARMUP_RUNS, measured_runs: measured },\n    runs,\n    aggregate,\n    methodology:\n      \"ink-testing-library render + rerender loop (one event-loop tick between \" +\n      \"rerenders so Ink flushes); per-frame ms = wall time distributed evenly \" +\n      \"across stdout.frames deltas (react-ink heuristic — includes tick \" +\n      \"overhead, not true per-frame instrumentation); FORCE_COLOR inherited \" +\n      \"from the invoking shell; 1 discarded warmup + N measured runs; \" +\n      \"std_dev is population std dev over run-level means.\",\n  };\n  const outPath = join(\n    dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),\n    \"..\",\n    \"docs\",\n    \"benchmarks\",\n    \"m0-chat-message-baseline.json\",\n  );\n  mkdirSync(dirname(outPath), { recursive: true });\n  writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(baseline, null, 2) + \"\\n\");\n  console.log(\n    `baseline written: docs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json`,\n  );\n}\n---PKG---\n{\n  \"name\": \"@theokit/tui\",\n  \"version\": \"0.0.0\",\n  \"description\": \"TUI component library for AI-agent surfaces \\u2014 Ink primitives for chat, tool-calls, agent timelines, diffs and metrics.\",\n  \"license\": \"Apache-2.0\",\n  \"repository\": {\n    \"type\": \"git\",\n    \"url\": \"git+https://github.com/usetheodev/theokit-tui.git\"\n  },\n  \"type\": \"module\",\n  \"exports\": {\n    \".\": {\n      \"types\": \"./dist/index.d.ts\",\n      \"default\": \"./dist/index.js\"\n    }\n  },\n  \"main\": \"./dist/index.js\",\n  \"types\": \"./dist/index.d.ts\",\n  \"files\": [\n    \"dist\"\n  ],\n  \"sideEffects\": false,\n  \"engines\": {\n    \"node\": \">=20\"\n  },\n  \"packageManager\": \"pnpm@10.34.1\",\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"build\": \"tsup\",\n    \"test\": \"vitest run\",\n    \"test:watch\": \"vitest\",\n    \"test:coverage\": \"vitest run --coverage\",\n    \"typecheck\": \"tsc --noEmit\",\n    \"lint\": \"eslint .\",\n    \"format\": \"prettier --write .\",\n    \"format:check\": \"prettier --check .\",\n    \"bench\": \"tsx benchmarks/run.ts\",\n    \"example\": \"tsx examples/basic.tsx\",\n    \"gates\": \"pnpm format:check && pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test && pnpm build\"\n  },\n  \"peerDependencies\": {\n    \"react\": \"^18.0.0 || ^19.0.0\"\n  },\n  \"dependencies\": {\n    \"ink\": \"^5.2.0\"\n  },\n  \"devDependencies\": {\n    \"@eslint/js\": \"^9\",\n    \"@types/node\": \"^22.20.0\",\n {"parentUuid":"8da89e3c-2d0e-4b02-88b4-ee4726fa6ad8","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a64aa7b00cab1e654","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_01VyJCyyCkyuhZyKBdziMWuP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01LLEASpqWeWx1psTJbfkVwn","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/paulo/Projetos/usetheo/theokit-tools/theokit-tui/.claude/agents/review-m0-walking-skeleton-2026-07-06/findings/domain-testing.yml","content":"agent: review-m0-walking-skeleton-domain-testing\nreview_target: main..HEAD for plan m0-walking-skeleton\ndomain: testing\ndomain_specific_patterns_checked:\n  [\n    \"snapshot-determinism-env-pins (ADR D2 / EC-5: FORCE_COLOR=1 + NO_COLOR neutralized + CI neutralized in vitest.config.ts)\",\n    \"fresh-subprocess NO_COLOR probe (chalk pins color level at module load — in-process stubEnv insufficient)\",\n    \"double-assertion strategy (toMatchSnapshot + plain-text toContain per behavior, ADR D2)\",\n    \"Box-width control on width-sensitive snapshots (ink-ui idiom)\",\n    \"single renderFrame determinism point (tests/helpers.tsx, react-ink idiom)\",\n    \"benchmark statistical protocol honesty (>= 3 runs, mean ± std dev, Number.isFinite oracle — EC-2)\",\n    \"zero-frame guard in bench harness (EC-2 MUST FIX: exit(1), never serialize NaN)\",\n    \"pyramid balance + edge-vs-negative lenses (rules/testing.md § 2, § 4.1)\",\n    \"typed-error negative case asserting exact message (rules/error-handling.md § 2 + testing.md § 4.1)\",\n    \"passWithNoTests: false fail-fast gate\",\n  ]\nfindings:\n  - id: F-dom-1\n    severity: MEDIUM\n    file: benchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx\n    line: 148\n    plan_ref: ADR D6/D9 — benchmark baseline is the milestone's runtime-evidence artifact; M1+ regress against the committed JSON\n    domain_anchor: plan ADR D2 (determinism root — color-detection inputs pinned) applied to tests but NOT to the bench harness\n    summary: Bench color environment is unpinned (\"FORCE_COLOR inherited from the invoking shell\" per the methodology field) — frame payload size (ANSI vs plain) varies with TTY/CI/shell, so M1 regression comparisons against this baseline can be skewed by environment, not code\n    evidence: |\n      benchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx methodology string:\n        \"FORCE_COLOR inherited from the invoking shell\"\n      benchmarks/run.ts spawnSync has no env pin:\n        spawnSync(\"pnpm\", [\"exec\", \"tsx\", join(benchDir, file), ...args], { stdio: \"inherit\" })\n      Contrast with vitest.config.ts, which pins FORCE_COLOR=1/NO_COLOR=\"\"/CI=\"\" precisely\n      because unpinned color detection is nondeterministic (ADR D2 / EC-5). The same class\n      of nondeterminism is left open in the harness whose output is the committed baseline.\n      Honesty credit: the methodology field discloses it (Rule 3 respected) — but disclosure\n      does not make future same-machine/different-shell comparisons apples-to-apples\n      (e.g., `pnpm bench` in a TTY vs `pnpm bench | tee` vs CI where CI=true flips supports-color).\n    recommended_action: Pin the color env in benchmarks/run.ts spawn (e.g., env {...process.env, FORCE_COLOR \"1\", NO_COLOR \"\"}) and record the pinned value in the baseline JSON (e.g., env.force_color field); regenerate the baseline once. Nothing recomputes\n      aggregate from runs; a corrupted-but-finite committed JSON stays green forever.\n    recommended_action: \"Strengthen cheaply: recompute mean of runs[].mean_ms_per_frame in the test and assert |recomputed − aggregate.mean| < epsilon; assert std_dev >= 0. Exemplary defense.\n        2. CI=\"\" cannot break vitest's own CI snapshot strictness on GitHub Actions:\n           test.env applies inside workers, and std-env isCI also reads GITHUB_ACTIONS,\n           which is untouched — missing snapshots still fail (not silently created) in CI.\n    recommended_action: Keep as-is (the canary is the real guard) but add a one-line comment noting NO_COLOR=\"\"/CI=\"\" relies on the modern \"non-empty\" reading of the spec + FORCE_COLOR precedence, so a future upgrade that flips the canary red is diagnosable in seconds.\n\n  - id: F-dom-4\n    severity: LOW\n    file: src/theme.tsx\n    line: 65\n    plan_ref: T1.1 (theme stub) — plan mandated boundary validation only for ChatMessage.role (EC-1 MUST FIX)\n    domain_anchor: rules/error-handling.md § 2 (validate at the boundary) + rules/testing.md § 4.1 (negative lens)\n    summary: Negative-lens asymmetry — ChatMessage.role has an exemplary negative case (typed TypeError + exact message asserted) but TheoTUIProvider's theme prop has zero negative cases;
